🔥 Nickel. P6 Music Vote V1 est livré (Spotify + Apple Music + YouTube Music + SoundCloud) avec exactement la bonne stratégie “universelle” : vote + winner + open link, et DJ override sécurisé.

La suite logique pour rendre ça vraiment “soirée-ready” : PR26 = “Rounds + Now Playing + timer + DJ controls”.
Parce qu’en vrai, en soirée, il faut :

des fenêtres de vote (ex: toutes les 3 min)

un winner verrouillé jusqu’au prochain round

un contrôle DJ/owner simple : Start round / End round / Skip / Force / Clear

et une UI “Now playing / Next up” claire.

Prompt Codex — PR26 (P6.2 Musique : Rounds + timer + Now Playing + DJ Panel)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P6 Music Vote V1 (vote universel + DJ override + 4 providers) est livré. Prochaine étape: PR26 = P6.2 “Rounds + Now Playing + DJ Panel” pour rendre le module utilisable en vraie soirée.

OBJECTIF PR26
- Ajouter un système de rounds (fenêtres de vote) event-scoped:
  - Owner/DJ démarre un round (durée configurable: 60/120/180/300s)
  - Les users votent pendant le round
  - À la fin (auto ou manual close), winner est “locked”
  - UI Now Playing / Next Winner / Next round timer
- Ajouter un panneau DJ/Owner (dans l’app, event-scoped):
  - Start round / End round
  - Set “Now Playing” (optionnel, via link/candidate)
  - Skip winner (clear winner and reopen round)
  - Force track (déjà) + Clear votes (déjà)
- Garder provider-agnostic: toujours “Open in …” (pas d’auto queue obligatoire)
- Respect runtime + network gating: offline/degraded/read-only => aucun write
- Respect DJ override flag.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Write-sensible: idempotency + audit sur start/end/nowPlaying/skip
- Anti-abus:
  - vote cooldown déjà présent
  - empêcher votes hors round si enabledRoundOnly=true (v1 yes)
- Module OFF => 404 MODULE_OFF (strict) + aucune UI
- Performance: polling léger:
  - users: 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline
  - admin panel: 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline

BACKEND — DATA MODEL
- Ajouter état de session (event_music_session):
  - event_id
  - enabled
  - dj_override_enabled
  - allow_mixed
  - provider_default
  - round:
      round_id (uuid)
      status: OPEN|LOCKED|IDLE
      started_at
      ends_at
      duration_sec
      locked_at
  - now_playing: TrackCandidate? (provider/url/title/artist/artwork)
  - winner: TrackCandidate? + locked_at + votesCount
  - last_updated_at
- Conserver votes/candidates existants; ajouter:
  - event_music_round_idempotency table (or reuse admin idempotency with action keys)

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
A) GET /events/{event_id}/music
   - enrichir réponse:
     { enabled, round{status, startedAt, endsAt, durationSec}, nowPlaying?, winner?, topCandidates[], lastUpdatedAt }

B) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/round/start
   body: { durationSec } (allowed values: 60/120/180/300)
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if djOverrideEnabled
   guard: runtime allows writes
   behavior:
     - creates new round_id
     - status OPEN
     - ends_at = now + duration
     - clears votes? (recommended yes: clear votes for new round)
     - audit

C) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/round/end
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - locks winner based on current votes
   - status LOCKED
   - sets winner + locked_at
   - audit

D) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/now-playing
   body: { candidate: TrackCandidate, reason? }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - sets now_playing
   - audit

E) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/skip
   body: { reason }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - clears winner and sets status IDLE (or re-open round if you prefer)
   - audit

F) Vote rule update:
- POST /events/{event_id}/music/vote should:
  - reject votes if round.status != OPEN (HTTP 409 VOTE_CLOSED)
  - or allow if you choose, but V2 should be round-only. Choose strict round-only.

G) (Optional) background auto-lock:
- simplest: no background job; client can call end round when timer reached.
- but GET /music should compute if now>ends_at and status OPEN => treat as LOCKED and lock lazily server-side (safe). Implement “lazy finalize” inside GET /music or on vote call.

TESTS BACKEND: tests/test_music_rounds.py
- start round idempotent
- vote rejected when no round open
- end round locks winner
- now_playing set requires permission
- DJ override respected
- runtime blocked writes return 403

FLUTTER — UI
1) MusicVoteScreen upgrades:
- Show:
  - Round status banner:
    - OPEN: countdown timer (endsAt - now)
    - LOCKED: “Winner locked”
    - IDLE: “Waiting for next round”
  - Now Playing card (if set) with “Open in …”
  - Winner card (if locked) with count + open link
  - Top candidates list + vote buttons:
    - enabled only when round OPEN and write gate ok
- Add “Admin/DJ panel” button visible only if user has permissions:
  - need permissions in /events/{id}/context:
    - canDjMusic (if staff dj role)
    - canAdminMusic (manager/owner)
  - If not available, derive from staff session role (if you store DJ role) or add to context now.

2) MusicAdminPanelScreen (new)
- Controls:
  - Start round duration selector (60/120/180/300)
  - End round
  - Set Now Playing (paste link or search)
  - Force track (reuse existing)
  - Clear votes (existing)
  - Skip winner
- All actions show pending state + reason required where specified.
- Gating: offline/degraded/read-only/runtime blocked disables.

3) Polling:
- Use same network provider:
  - online 2s, degraded 5s, offline stop
- Ensure dedup/no flicker:
  - keep last state, update diff.

E) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/pin (MOD+)
   body { pinned: bool, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
F) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/lock (MOD+)
   body { locked: bool, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
G) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/delete (MOD+)
   body { reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
Admin categories (ADMIN/OWNER):
H) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/categories
   body { yearId, name, orderIndex? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
I) PUT /committees/{committee_id}/forum/categories/{category_id}
   body { name?, orderIndex?, isArchived? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required

GUARDS / VALIDATIONS
- Ensure membership active and year belongs to committee
- Ensure category belongs to committee/year
- Soft delete: posts hidden from list but accessible to MOD/ADMIN for audit? V1: hide entirely for non-mod.
- Cursor pagination: can be created_at timestamp + id.

OBJECTIF PR29

  - CSV invités
  - CSV planning

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS (FINANCES)
A) GET /committees/{committee_id}/finances/ledger?year_id=...&cursor&limit
B) POST /committees/{committee_id}/finances/ledger

   audit

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS (BAPTÊME)

   body { yearId, name }

K) GET /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/invites?campaignId=...
   perms: can_manage_bapteme
L) POST /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/slots
   body { campaignId, startsAt, endsAt, location, description }
   Idempotency-Key required
M) POST /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/assign
   body { inviteId, slotId }
   Idempotency-Key required
N) POST /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/incidents
   body { campaignId, inviteId?, type, note }
   Idempotency-Key required
   perms: can_manage_bapteme (strict)
O) GET /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/export_invites.csv?campaignId=...
P) GET /committees/{committee_id}/bapteme/export_slots.csv?campaignId=...

OBJECTIF PR30 (P9.1)
- Garantir que TOUTES les actions write critiques respectent:
  1) Idempotency-Key obligatoire
  2) Audit log systématique (actor, event_id/committee_id/bar_id, action, payload minimal, timestamp)
  3) QR sensibles signés + expiration + vérification serveur stricte (resolve)
- Appliquer ce hardening sans casser les flows existants (USER/ORGA/BAR/COMITÉ).
- Ajouter des tests ciblés qui vérifient “pas de write sans idempotency” + “audit présent” sur les endpoints critiques.

SCOPE EXACT (CRITIQUE)
Endpoints write sensibles à hardener (si pas déjà fait partout, vérifier et corriger):

A) EVENT
- /events/{id}/join, /events/join_by_code (idempotency + audit)
- entry staff /events/{id}/entry/checkin (déjà)
- orders create (panier -> order) (idempotency + audit)
- polymarket buy/create/resolve (déjà mais vérifier partout)
- music vote/admin rounds (déjà)
- broadcasts create (déjà)
- incidents create/action (déjà)
- profitability costs update (déjà)

B) BAR MODE
- orders transitions accept/ready/claim/cancel (déjà)
- menu admin writes (déjà)

C) COMMITTEE
- join_by_code, invite_codes, role change, revoke (déjà)
- forum create/mod/category admin (déjà)
- finances ledger create + budgets put (déjà)
- bapteme creates/assign/incidents (déjà)

=> Ton job est d’AUDITER globalement et de combler les trous: toute route write doit exiger Idempotency-Key + audit. Si un endpoint write est volontairement “non critique”, documenter pourquoi.

QR SIGNATURES (CAPSTONE)
- Il existe déjà des QR pack signés (orga events QR pack, staff QR, gift QR).
- Étendre la vérification serveur:
  - /qr/resolve doit vérifier signature + expiry + token lookup + revoked pour tous types sensibles:
    JOIN_EVENT, ENTRY_STAFF, BAR_STAFF, MENU, GIFT_RECEIVE, (et tout autre type existant)
  - Si QR non signé => invalid (sauf QR legacy explicite)
- Ajouter une clé de signature stable (env var) + rotation simple (optional)
- Ajouter des tests QR resolve couvrant:
  - expired => expired
  - signature invalid => invalid
  - revoked => revoked
  - ok => ok

AUDIT LOGS
- Assurer que l’audit log contient:
  - actor_user_id
  - scope: event_id OR committee_id OR bar_id
  - action string stable
  - idempotency_key (si présent)
  - created_at
  - minimal payload (pas de données sensibles en clair pour baptême incidents: store only incident_id + type)
- Si tu as déjà un audit model/table, réutilise.
- Sinon créer un EventAuditLog global.

IDEMPOTENCY RULES
- Toute route write doit:
  - refuser si Idempotency-Key manquant (400 IDEMPOTENCY_REQUIRED)
  - stocker réponse stable (status + body minimal) quand re-played
- Harmoniser pattern (helper unique).

LIVRABLES TECHNIQUES
1) Backend: audit global
- Faire un script interne ou une checklist dans docs (optionnel), mais surtout corriger le code.
- Ajouter helper ensure_idempotency_key(request) + store/replay.

2) Backend: QR resolve hardening
- Centraliser validate_signed_qr(payload) dans un service.
- Appliquer sur tous types sensibles.

3) Backend: tests capstone
- tests/test_capstone_write_sensible.py:
  - vérifie que certains endpoints clés refusent sans Idempotency-Key
  - vérifie qu’un audit log est créé quand write OK
- tests/test_qr_resolve_signed.py:
  - couverture des statuts ok/expired/invalid/revoked pour 2-3 types

4) App/Portal
- Pas de refactor UI massif.
- Ajuster seulement si un endpoint commence à refuser sans Idempotency-Key:
  - s’assurer que le client envoie toujours un Idempotency-Key sur ces calls (si trous)
- Do not change UX flows.

🔥 Parfait. P9.1 est livré comme il faut : idempotency obligatoire, audit enrichi (actor + scope + key), QR signing centralisé + rotation, /qr/resolve strict (avec escape hatch legacy), tests capstone ✅.

Prochaine étape recommandée (terrain + adoption immédiate) : P8.1 WiFi via QR

C’est ultra utile en soirée / chapi : tu affiches un QR “WiFi event”, le public scanne et obtient un token / credentials (ou un portail) avec lifecycle + revoke. Et ça respecte parfaitement ton capstone P9.

Prompt Codex — PR31 (P8.1 WiFi via QR : token lifecycle, revoke, offline read-only)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P9.1 capstone write-sensible est livré. Prochaine étape: PR31 = P8.1 “WiFi via QR” (module indépendant ON/OFF).

OBJECTIF PR31
- Ajouter un module WiFi event-scoped accessible via QR:
  - ORGA génère un QR WiFi (signed + expiring) dans le QR pack event
  - USER scanne -> obtient soit:
    A) un portail “WiFi info” (SSID + password) si autorisé
    B) OU un token temporaire (recommended) avec expiration
  - ORGA peut REVOKE un token (lifecycle)
- Respect strict:
  - module OFF => aucune UI + resolve renvoie module_off
  - offline => mode lecture (pas de génération/revoke, mais lecture cache)
  - write-sensible: idempotency + audit log
  - QR signed + expiry (utiliser qr_signing_service introduit PR30)

SCOPE V1 (réaliste)
- Pas d’intégration routeur/Unifi, pas de config WiFi automatique OS-level.
- Juste “distribution contrôlée” des infos via QR + token.
- Option: afficher “SSID + pass” seulement si token valide, sinon refuser.

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_wifi_settings:
  - event_id
  - enabled (bool)
  - ssid (string)
  - password (string) (store as encrypted? V1: plain in DB but never in logs)
  - instructions (optional)
  - updated_at, updated_by
- event_wifi_tokens:
  - id
  - event_id
  - token_hash (sha256)
  - created_at
  - expires_at
  - revoked_at, revoked_by, revoke_reason
  - issued_to_user_id (optional)
- event_wifi_idempotency (or reuse admin idempotency)

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Admin ORGA (manager/owner):
A) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi
   returns { enabled, ssid, hasPassword(true/false), instructions, tokenStats(active/revoked/expired) }
B) PUT /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi
   body { enabled, ssid?, password?, instructions? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - audit log (do NOT log password value)
C) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi/tokens
   body { ttlSeconds } default 3600, allowed 300..86400
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - returns { tokenId, token, expiresAt }
   - audit (no token in audit)
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi/tokens/{token_id}/revoke
   body { reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - audit

Public USER (event-scoped):
E) GET /events/{event_id}/wifi/claim
   query: token=...
   - validates: module ON, token exists, not expired, not revoked
   - returns { ssid, password, instructions, expiresAt }
   - privacy: do not expose other tokens; just this claim response
   - rate-limit claim attempts

QR INTEGRATION
- Add a new QrType: WIFI_EVENT
- ORGA QR pack includes a signed deeplink:
  tore://qr?type=wifi_event&eventId=...&expiresAt=...&sig=...
- /qr/resolve for WIFI_EVENT must enforce signed QR validation (using PR30 services)
- When USER scans WIFI_EVENT:
  - App routes to WifiScreen:
    - fetches event wifi claim flow (needs a token)
  - BUT the QR itself should not include the password.
  - Flow:
    1) QR opens WifiLandingScreen with “Request WiFi access”
    2) ORGA generates tokens and displays another QR “WiFi token” OR shares token string
    => Too complex.
  - Better v1:
    - WIFI_EVENT QR itself carries a short-lived token generated in advance.
    - So QR pack includes WIFI token QR (rotated by ORGA as needed)
    - Implement this as: ORGA endpoint creates token + returns a QR payload with token embedded.
    - QR type: WIFI_TOKEN (signed) with tokenId + token + expiry.

Therefore:
- Add QrType.WIFI_TOKEN, payload includes eventId + token (or tokenId + token) + expiresAt + sig
- /qr/resolve must validate signature and then validate token lifecycle.

Update QR pack generation:
- include WIFI_TOKEN QR only if wifi module enabled
- generate 1 default token TTL 6h (configurable later)

TESTS BACKEND
- tests/test_wifi_qr_tokens.py
  - module off behavior
  - create token idempotent
  - claim ok, expired, revoked
  - audit logs created without password/token leakage
  - qr resolve signed enforcement for WIFI_TOKEN

PORTAL UI (ORGA)
- In EventControlHub add tile “WiFi”
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/wifi:
  - enable toggle
  - ssid + password fields
  - “Generate QR token” (ttl select)
  - list latest tokens (status active/expired/revoked) + revoke
  - copy deeplink/QR payload for printing
  - write gating offline/degraded/runtime

FLUTTER UI (USER)
- Add WifiScreen:
  - opened from QR router for WIFI_TOKEN
  - calls /events/{event_id}/wifi/claim?token=...
  - displays SSID + password + instructions
  - shows expiry countdown (optional)
  - offline: if cached last claim, show cached with “mode lecture”
- Ensure module ON/OFF:
  - if wifi module OFF, QR router goes to ModuleUnavailableScreen

SECURITY
- Never log wifi password or raw token in audit logs.
- Store token as hash in DB.
- Rate limit claim endpoint.

DoD PR31
- ORGA peut activer WiFi, config SSID/pass, générer QR token, révoquer.
- USER scan -> voit WiFi info si token valide.
- Signed QR enforced + lifecycle enforced.
- Tests pass.

- PR title: `feat(event): wifi via QR tokens + revoke (P8.1)`

TESTING

- Portal: npm run build
- App: diff --check (+ flutter analyze if possible)

Prompt Codex — PR32 (P8.2 Hunts QR : création, scan, rewards, classement, defend)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P8.1 WiFi QR est livré. Prochaine étape: PR32 = P8.2 “Hunts QR + cagnottes défendables” (module indépendant ON/OFF, event-scoped).

OBJECTIF PR32 (Hunt V1 jouable)
- Un event peut activer le module Hunt (modulesOn.hunt=true).
- ORGA crée une “Hunt” (une chasse) avec:
  - titre, description, période active, règles
  - une liste de checkpoints QR (ex: 10 QR cachés)
  - chaque checkpoint donne des points + éventuellement une récompense (tickets / badge)
- USER scanne un QR checkpoint:
  - validation signée + expiry (QR sensibles)
  - idempotent: un user ne peut valider 2 fois le même checkpoint
  - retour immédiat “+X points” + feedback <1s
- Leaderboard Hunt:
  - top3 + list dense + me
- Defendable pot (v1 simple):
  - chaque user possède un “pot” de points de la hunt
  - une action “steal/defend” possible 1 fois / 10 min:
    - user A peut tenter de voler une fraction (ex 10%) à user B si B est “visible” (ou via QR duel)
  - V1 safe: pas de P2P en offline, runtime guards stricts, audit logs

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Module OFF => aucune UI + endpoints 404 MODULE_OFF
- Write-sensible:
  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire sur scan checkpoint + actions steal/defend + creation admin
  - Audit logs sur toutes actions
- QR: signés + expiration + resolve strict (utiliser qr_signing_service / qr_validation_service)
- Réseau:
  - offline => mode lecture (pas de scan write)
  - degraded => bloquer writes critiques (scan & steal sont critiques)
- Performance terrain:
  - scan -> feedback UX <1s (optimiser backend path, minimal writes)
- Privacy:
  - leaderboard masque les users private (Anonymous-xxxx), mais “me” toujours visible

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_hunts:
  - id, event_id, title, description, starts_at, ends_at, is_active
  - rules_text
  - created_by, created_at
- event_hunt_checkpoints:
  - id, hunt_id, label, points, reward_tickets (optional)
  - qr_token_id (links generated tokens)
  - order_index
- event_hunt_claims:
  - id, hunt_id, checkpoint_id, user_id
  - claimed_at
  - points_awarded
- event_hunt_scores:
  - hunt_id, user_id, score (cached aggregate), last_updated_at
- event_hunt_actions_idempotency (or reuse existing idempotency service with scope keys)
- event_hunt_steals:
  - id, hunt_id, attacker_user_id, victim_user_id
  - amount, created_at, result (OK|BLOCKED|COOLDOWN|VICTIM_PRIVATE)
  - audit

QR TYPES
- Add QrType.HUNT_CHECKPOINT (signed)
Payload: { type=hunt_checkpoint, eventId, huntId, checkpointId, tokenId, expiresAt, sig }
- /qr/resolve must enforce signed validation for hunt_checkpoint
- QR pack generation for Hunt should be in ORGA portal hunt admin (not in event create pack by default).

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Admin ORGA (manager/owner):
A) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/hunts
   body { title, description, startsAt, endsAt, rulesText, checkpoints:[{label,points,rewardTickets?,orderIndex}] }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - creates hunt + checkpoints
   - generates signed QR tokens per checkpoint (TTL default endsAt)
   - audit
B) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/hunts
C) GET /partner/portal/orga/hunts/{hunt_id}/qr_pack
   returns list {checkpoint,label,points,deeplink,expiresAt} + printable view in portal
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/hunts/{hunt_id}/close
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - sets inactive, audit

USER:
E) GET /events/{event_id}/hunts/active
   returns current hunt + brief checkpoints count + endsAt + myScore
F) POST /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/claim
   body { checkpointId, token }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - validates module ON
   - validates token hash + expiry + not revoked (like wifi)
   - ensures checkpoint belongs to hunt+event
   - idempotent: if already claimed -> return stable response (alreadyClaimed=true)
   - updates score aggregate
   - returns { ok, pointsAwarded, totalScore, rewardTicketsAwarded? }
   - audit
G) GET /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/leaderboard
   returns top3 + list + me (mask privacy)

H) POST /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/steal
   body { victimUserId }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - cooldown 10 min per attacker
   - deny if victim privacy=private (unless victim==me)
   - stealAmount = floor(victimScore*0.1) min 1 max 50
   - update both scores
   - audit
   - returns { ok, amount, attackerScore, victimScoreMasked? }

TESTS BACKEND: tests/test_hunt_v1.py
- module off strict
- create hunt idempotent
- claim ok + idempotent replay
- claim invalid/expired token
- leaderboard masking
- steal cooldown + victim private blocked
- audit logs exist

PORTAL UI
- Add tile “Hunt” in EventControlHub if module ON
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/hunts:
  - list hunts + create hunt wizard (simple form + add checkpoints)
  - after create => show QR pack list + copy + print-friendly
  - close hunt button
  - offline write gate

FLUTTER UI (USER)
- EventHub shows “Hunt” module card when ON.
- HuntScreen:
  - shows active hunt, countdown, myScore
  - CTA “Scan checkpoint” opens universal scanner (strict write gating)
  - recent claims list (optional)
  - leaderboard button opens LeaderboardScreen
- Claim flow:
  - QR router recognizes hunt_checkpoint and routes directly to claim handler (or opens HuntScreen and auto-claims)
  - show success feedback big (+points), haptic, sound
  - handle alreadyClaimed state nicely
- LeaderboardScreen:
  - reuse ranking UI pattern (top3 + list dense + me highlight)
- All writes blocked offline/degraded/read-only/runtime.

DoD PR32
- ORGA peut créer hunt + obtenir pack QR
- USER peut claim checkpoints via scan
- Scores/leaderboard fonctionnent
- Steal v1 fonctionne avec cooldown et privacy mask
- Tests pass

Prompt Codex — PR33 (Flutter USER : Hunt jouable + scan + feedback + leaderboard)

  1) Event Hub affiche module “Hunt” quand modulesOn.hunt=true.

     - scan checkpoint QR -> claim -> feedback big (+points)
     - gérer alreadyClaimed proprement

- Offline:
  - cache read-only (active hunt + leaderboard + dernier score)
  - si offline: afficher cached + “mode lecture”

  - claim doit donner un feedback immédiat (haptique + snack + card).

CONTRAINTES

- Ne pas casser QR router universel.

  - OFFLINE => blocage writes
  - DEGRADED => blocage writes critiques (claim/steal = critical)
  - runtime event => bloquant si PAUSE/MENU_ONLY/DEGRADED/FROZEN/CLOSED (selon règles existantes)
- Module OFF => aucune UI (déjà filtré par EventHub modules ON-only)

IMPLEMENTATION FLUTTER (structure)
A) API client (lib/api.dart)
- Ajouter méthodes manquantes si absentes:
  - getActiveHunt(eventId)
  - claimHunt(eventId, huntId, checkpointId, token) (Idempotency-Key)
  - getHuntLeaderboard(eventId, huntId)
  - stealHunt(eventId, huntId, victimUserId) (Idempotency-Key)
- Ajouter modèles typed pour réponses.

B) Providers (Riverpod)
- activeHuntProvider(eventId) polling (2s online, 5s degraded, stop offline)
- huntLeaderboardProvider(eventId, huntId) polling léger (5s online, 10s degraded)
- local cache SharedPreferences:
  - cache key: hunt_active_{eventId}
  - hunt_lb_{eventId}_{huntId}
  - store payload + lastSyncAt + TTL 24h

C) UI Screens
1) HuntScreen (route depuis EventHub)
- Header: Hunt title + countdown + chip ACTIVE/ENDED
- ToreCard “My score” (score + lastClaim info)
- CTA primary “Scan checkpoint”
  - opens universal QrScannerScreen
  - on scan: if payload type hunt_checkpoint:
      - validate eventId matches active event (sinon message)
      - call claim endpoint immediately
      - show ClaimResultSheet (big)
  - If scanned QR not hunt_checkpoint: show QrStatusScreen (existing)
- Secondary: button “Leaderboard”

2) ClaimResultSheet
- success: big +points + totalScore
- alreadyClaimed: show “Déjà validé” + totalScore
- error: show reason (expired/invalid/revoked/offline/runtime blocked)
- Add haptic + sound (reuse existing)

3) HuntLeaderboardScreen
- Top3 cards (premium)
- List dense (ToreListRow) with “Steal” trailing action button for each row (except me)
- When press Steal:
  - confirm dialog
  - call steal endpoint
  - show result snack (+amount or error)
  - cooldown UI: disable button if backend says cooldownRemaining (if not provided, just show error message)

4) QR Router integration
- When scanning hunt_checkpoint anywhere (global scanner):
  - route to HuntClaim handler:
    - if no active event context or mismatch => show QrInvalidScreen with explanation
    - else push HuntScreen and auto-run claim (or show modal claim result directly)
- Prefer: open modal claim result without deep navigation if user already in event.

D) EventHub wiring
- Ensure event_hub_screen.dart module card opens HuntScreen when key 'hunt' ON.
- Keep modules ON-only rule.

TESTING

Prompt Codex — PR34 (P8 Battles/Affonds V1 : duels, rounds, votes, ranking)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. Hunt est complet. Prochaine étape: PR34 = P8 “Battles / Affonds” V1 (module folklore), event-scoped, ON/OFF strict.

OBJECTIF PR34
- Un module Battles (alias “Affonds”) qui permet:
  1) ORGA/DJ lance une “battle round” (OPEN/LOCKED/IDLE) avec thème + durée.
  2) Users participent en 1 tap pendant OPEN:
     - soit “Je participe” (simple)
     - soit “Vote” (TEAM A / TEAM B) si round est de type duel
  3) Résultat affiché live + winner + historique court.
  4) Classement battles (score) : top3 + liste dense + me.
- Option folklore: “Duc de l’Affond” = badge du #1 du round, affiché dans UI.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Module OFF => endpoints 404 MODULE_OFF et aucune entrée UI.
- Writes critiques (start/end/vote/participate/force winner/clear) :
  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire (utiliser ensure_idempotency_key)
  - audit logs systématiques
  - bloqués offline/degraded/runtime blocked (PAUSE/MENU_ONLY/DEGRADED/FROZEN/CLOSED)
- Privacy:
  - leaderboard masque users private (Anonymous-xxxx), me visible.
- Performance:
  - UX vote <1s: backend path minimal.
- Offline:
  - read-only avec cache (round state + last results + leaderboard)

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_battle_rounds:
  - id, event_id
  - title, kind: PARTICIPATION|DUEL
  - optionA_label, optionB_label (for DUEL)
  - status: IDLE|OPEN|LOCKED
  - starts_at, ends_at, locked_at
  - created_by, created_at
  - winner (A|B|NONE) + winner_count_a/b
- event_battle_votes:
  - id, round_id, user_id
  - choice: A|B|PARTICIPATE
  - created_at
  - unique(round_id,user_id) (idempotence logical)
- event_battle_scores:
  - event_id, user_id, score, last_updated_at
  - scoring v1:
    - participation in OPEN => +1
    - correct winner vote (DUEL) => +2 (if vote matches winner)
    - participation in winning side => +1 extra
- event_battle_idempotency
- (optional) event_battle_actions log table if needed; but audit service should cover actions.

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
USER:
A) GET /events/{event_id}/battles/state
   returns current round (if any), serverTime, myChoice, counts, status, endsAt
B) POST /events/{event_id}/battles/vote
   body { roundId, choice } where choice in [A,B,PARTICIPATE]
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - module guard
   - runtime guard
   - only allowed if round OPEN and now<endsAt
   - upsert vote (unique constraint)
   - returns { ok, myChoice, countsA, countsB, status }
C) GET /events/{event_id}/battles/leaderboard
   returns top3 + list + me + rulesText

ADMIN ORGA/DJ:
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/start
   body { title, kind, durationSeconds, optionALabel?, optionBLabel? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - closes previous OPEN (lock)
   - starts new OPEN
   - audit (no sensitive)
E) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/lock
   body { roundId }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - lock now, compute winner for DUEL (max votes)
   - update scores (batch)
   - audit
F) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/force_winner
   body { roundId, winner:A|B|NONE, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - recompute scores accordingly
   - audit (reason required)
G) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/history?limit=20
H) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/clear
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - clears votes for current round (optional v1) or resets to IDLE
   - audit (reason required)

TESTS BACKEND: tests/test_battles_v1.py
- module off strict
- start round idempotent
- vote allowed only OPEN and before expiry
- lock computes winner + updates scores
- leaderboard masking privacy
- runtime blocked write behavior
- audit logs created
- idempotency required on write endpoints

PORTAL UI (ORGA)
- Add tile “Battles / Affonds” in EventControlHub if module ON
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/battles:
  - current round card (status + countdown + counts)
  - start round form:
      - kind: Participation vs Duel
      - title + duration
      - if duel: labels A/B
  - actions:
      - Lock round
      - Force winner (reason)
      - View history (last 10)
  - write gating offline/degraded/runtime
  - polling 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline

FLUTTER UI (USER)
- Event Hub card “Battles” opens BattlesScreen.
- BattlesScreen:
  - shows current round (title, timer, status chip)
  - if OPEN:
      - if kind PARTICIPATION: CTA “JE PARTICIPE”
      - if kind DUEL: 2 big buttons TEAM A / TEAM B (meme style mais clean)
  - show live counts (bar/progress)
  - show last winner card when LOCKED
  - link to BattlesLeaderboardScreen
- BattlesLeaderboardScreen:
  - reuse ranking layout (top3 + list dense + me highlight + rulesText)
- Providers:
  - battlesStateProvider polling 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline + cache TTL 24h
  - battlesLeaderboardProvider polling 10s online / 20s degraded + cache TTL 24h
- Writes blocked via write_gate critical
- QR: (optional) later add QrType.BATTLE_JOIN; NOT in V1 to keep scope tight.

DoD PR34

   - audit

   body { type: OK|PANIC }
   Idempotency-Key required
   - creates checkin row
   - returns { ok, createdAt }
   - audit
D) GET /events/{event_id}/safety/history?limit=50
   - returns my history + buddy last status (minimal)

   body { assigneeUserId? }
   Idempotency-Key required
   - audit

   Idempotency-Key required
   - audit

I) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/safety/export.json

   - lastSyncAt visible
4) Writes:
   - checkin/panic/buddy sont CRITIQUES => bloqués offline/degraded/read-only/runtime
   - Idempotency-Key obligatoire sur POST
   - feedback terrain: haptic + snack + update immédiate UI

CONTRAINTES
- Utiliser le write gate unifié (WriteIntent.critical)
- Respecter runtime_write_guard / event state banner
- UI avec composants TORÈ (ToreCard/ToreCTA/ToreChip/ToreDivider/ToreListRow)
- Pas de diffusion ticker publique (rien à faire côté app, juste ne pas ajouter)

FLUTTER — API (lib/api.dart)
Ajouter si absent:
- getSafetyState(eventId)

Créer modèles typés:
- SafetyState (enabled, orgaPhone?, messageHint?, myBuddy?, myLastCheckin?, buddyLastCheckin?)

FLUTTER — PROVIDERS (Riverpod)

   polling 5s online / 10s degraded / stop offline
   cache SharedPreferences TTL 24h
- safetyHistoryProvider(eventId):
   fetch + cache TTL 24h
- helper cacheStatus (isCached, lastSyncAt)
- invalidate providers after successful write

Summary
Ajout de l’API Safe Kit côté Flutter dans Api : getSafetyState, setSafetyBuddy (avec Idempotency-Key), safetyCheckin (avec Idempotency-Key) et getSafetyHistory, avec parsing typé des réponses Safety.

Prochaine étape (PR37) — Carte & itinéraires + Calendrier guindaille (P8)

Je te donne un prompt PR37 qui reste V1 utile sans tomber dans Google Maps full stack.

Prompt Codex — PR37 (P8 : Carte & itinéraires + calendrier V1, offline-friendly)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. Hunt + Battles + WiFi + Safe Kit + Comité OS + Music Vote sont livrés. Prochaine étape: PR37 = P8 “Carte & itinéraires + calendrier guindaille” V1.

OBJECTIF PR37 (V1 utile, adoption)
USER:
1) Module “Carte” dans Event Hub si modulesOn.map=true
   - affiche le lieu event (adresse + lat/lng si dispo)
   - boutons: Google Maps / Apple Maps / Waze / TEC (lien)
   - section “Navettes” (si configuré): départ/retour + last shuttle time
2) Module “Calendrier” accessible depuis Home (hors event) ou comité:
   - liste d’events publics (Live/Today/Upcoming) cross-bars
   - filtre: “Aujourd’hui / Semaine / Mois”
   - ajout au calendrier device (ICS) + partage
3) Offline:
   - mode lecture + cache 24h (event location + calendar list)
4) Scope strict:
   - pas de géolocalisation temps réel
   - pas de navigation turn-by-turn in-app
   - juste deep links vers apps externes + affichage infos

BACKEND (FastAPI) — endpoints minimal
A) Event location config:
   - GET /events/{event_id}/map
     returns { enabled, venueName?, address?, lat?, lng?, navettes?: { enabled, address?, lastReturnTime?, notes? }, tecLink?, wazeLink? }
   - Strict module OFF => 404 MODULE_OFF (modulesOn.map)
B) Public calendar:
   - GET /calendar/events?from=...&to=...&scope=public
     returns list of public events (id,name,startAt,endAt,venue,address,barId,barName,status)
   - pagination cursor optional
C) ICS:
   - GET /calendar/events/{event_id}.ics
     returns ICS text (VEVENT) with DTSTART/DTEND, location, summary, URL=deep link tore://event/{id}
   - no auth required if event is public
D) Portal ORGA config:
   - GET/PUT /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/map/settings
     Idempotency-Key required on PUT
     fields: enabled, venueName, address, lat,lng, navettes config, tecLink/wazeLink
     audit logs (do not log full address if you consider it sensitive; ok to log “updated map settings” with event_id)

MODELS
- event_map_settings (event_id, enabled, venue_name, address, lat, lng, tec_link, waze_link)
- event_navettes_settings (event_id, enabled, address, last_return_time, notes)
- idempotency table event_map_idempotency (or reuse generic)

TESTS backend: tests/test_map_calendar_v1.py
- module off strict for /events/{id}/map
- calendar list returns expected shape
- ics endpoint returns a VEVENT with DTSTART/DTEND/LOCATION/SUMMARY
- map settings PUT requires idempotency + audit

PORTAL
- Add tile “Carte & Navettes” in EventControlHub when module map ON
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/map:
  - toggle enabled
  - venue name + address + lat/lng (optional)
  - navettes toggle + address + last return time + notes
  - quick preview card
  - write gating offline/degraded/runtime
  - save uses Idempotency-Key

FLUTTER
A) API
- add getEventMap(eventId)
- add getCalendarEvents(from,to)
- add getEventIcsUrl(eventId) OR just open backend URL in browser
B) Providers
- eventMapProvider(eventId) with cache TTL 24h
- calendarEventsProvider(range) with cache TTL 24h
C) UI
1) EventMapScreen (from Event Hub module “Carte”):
   - ToreCard venue/address + chips status
   - buttons:
     - Ouvrir Google Maps (geo:)
     - Ouvrir Apple Plans
     - Ouvrir Waze
     - Ouvrir TEC link (if provided)
   - Navettes card (if enabled): address + lastReturnTime + notes
   - Offline banner + last sync
2) CalendarScreen (entry from HomeHub when no active event, and optionally from CommitteeHome):
   - tabs/chips Today / Week / Month
   - list cards (event name, time, bar)
   - CTA “Ajouter au calendrier” -> open ICS url (url_launcher)
   - CTA “Rejoindre” -> route to BarEvents or JoinEvent flow if you have direct event id join (if not, just open EventHub placeholder with read-only)

DEPENDENCIES
- Use url_launcher (already added for tel:). Reuse it for external map apps + ICS.
- For geo links:
  - Google: https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=lat,lng OR encoded address
  - Apple Maps: http://maps.apple.com/?q=... or ll=lat,lng
  - Waze: https://waze.com/ul?ll=lat,lng&navigate=yes
  - TEC: use tecLink if provided
